### EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Crime

<!-- module: EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Crime -->

Target: `EpidemicGameFrameworkCrime`.

- **Responsibility:** Owns crimes, legal records, warrants/sentences and legal history.

- **Public headers and types:** `EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Crime/include/Epidemic/GameFramework/Crime/crime.h` (`AuthorityId`, `AuthorityRecord`, `BountyRecord`, `BountyRecordId`, `BountyState`, `CrimeCandidate`, `CrimeCandidatePolicy`, `CrimeCandidateWitness`, `CrimeCaseState`, `CrimeChange`, `CrimeChangeBatch`, `CrimeChangeKind`, `CrimeDiagnostics`, `CrimeEvaluationResult`, `CrimeProofState`, `CrimeRecord`, `CrimeRecordId`, `CrimeService`, `CrimeSnapshot`, `CrimeTypeId`, `EvidenceId`, `EvidenceRecord`, `EvidenceTypeId`, `IdHash`, `JurisdictionId`, `JurisdictionRecord`, `LawDefinition`, `LawId`, `LawResponseDefinition`, `LawResponseId`, `LawResponseRecord`, `LawResponseRequest`, `LawResponseTypeId`, `LegalityPreview`, `WitnessRecord`, `WitnessRecordId`, `WitnessTypeId`)

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** `AuthorityId`, `BountyRecordId`, `CrimeRecordId`, `CrimeTypeId`, `EvidenceId`, `EvidenceTypeId`, `JurisdictionId`, `LawId`, `LawResponseId`, `LawResponseTypeId`, `TWrappedId`, `WitnessRecordId`, `WitnessTypeId`

### EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Effects

<!-- module: EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Effects -->

Target: `EpidemicGameFrameworkEffects`.

- **Responsibility:** Owns effect definitions, execution/deferred state and effect handlers.

- **Public headers and types:** `EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Effects/include/Epidemic/GameFramework/Effects/effects.h` (`DeferredEffectId`, `DeferredEffectIdHash`, `DeferredEffectPersistence`, `DeferredEffectRecord`, `EffectBatchDisposition`, `EffectChange`, `EffectChangeBatch`, `EffectChangeKind`, `EffectCommitDisposition`, `EffectCommitResult`, `EffectDefinition`, `EffectDefinitionId`, `EffectDefinitionIdHash`, `EffectExecutionBudget`, `EffectExecutionId`, `EffectExecutionPolicy`, `EffectExecutionResult`, `EffectHandlerCapabilities`, `EffectOperation`, `EffectOperationDisposition`, `EffectOperationResult`, `EffectPrepareDisposition`, `EffectPrepareResult`, `EffectRequest`, `EffectService`, `EffectStepDefinition`, `EffectTargetSelector`, `EffectTargetState`, `EffectTypeId`, `EffectTypeIdHash`, `EffectsDiagnostics`, `EffectsSnapshot`, `IEffectHandler`, `IEffectTargetStateProvider`, `PayloadValidator`, `RegisteredEffectPayload`)

- **Dependency list:** `EpidemicGameFrameworkFoundation`, `EpidemicFoundation`

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** `IEffectHandler`, `IEffectTargetStateProvider`

- **Authoritative state:** Module-private storage represented by its service/runtime/record types: `DeferredEffectRecord`, `EffectDefinition`, `EffectDefinitionId`, `EffectDefinitionIdHash`, `EffectService`, `EffectStepDefinition`, `EffectTargetState`, `EffectsSnapshot`, `IEffectTargetStateProvider`. Storage members discovered in declarations/implementations: `budget_exhaustions`, `changes`, `deferred`, `deferred_by_schedule`, `deferred_ids`, `definitions`, `derived_effects`, `execution_ids`, `executions`, `frozen`, `handlers`, `journal_epoch`, `next_change_sequence`, `operations`, `rejections`, `target_state_provider`, `targets`, `waves`.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** Types: `EffectBatchDisposition`, `EffectChangeBatch`, `EffectsDiagnostics`. Storage members: `journal_epoch`, `next_change_sequence`.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** `DeferredEffectId`, `EffectDefinitionId`, `EffectExecutionId`, `EffectTypeId`

- **State machines:** `EffectChangeKind`, `EffectTargetState`

- **Persistent and transient state:** Snapshot/checkpoint/archive carriers: `EffectsSnapshot`. Transient request/session/queue carriers: `EffectRequest`. Durability is claimed only where the snapshot/restore contract says so.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** `cb852bb4b771174f:epidemic::gameplay::effects::EffectService::CaptureSnapshot (EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Effects/include/Epidemic/GameFramework/Effects/effects.h:402)`, `80342834ccbe6b67:epidemic::gameplay::effects::EffectService::RestoreSnapshot (EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Effects/include/Epidemic/GameFramework/Effects/effects.h:403)`

- **Authoritative state:** Module-private storage represented by its service/runtime/record types: `ClueRecord`, `ConditionEvaluationState`, `ConsequenceExecutionState`, `JournalVisibilityState`, `NarrativeArcDefinition`, `NarrativeBeatDefinition`, `NarrativeChoiceState`, `NarrativeConditionDefinition`, `NarrativeConsequenceDefinition`, `NarrativeEventExecutionState`, `NarrativeGateState`, `NarrativeObjectiveDefinition`, `NarrativeObjectiveRuntimeState`, `NarrativeObjectiveState`, `NarrativeRuntimeState`, `NarrativeService`, `NarrativeSnapshot`, `NarrativeThreadDefinition`, `NarrativeThreadState`, `RumorRecord`, `RumorState`, `StoryletDefinition`, `StoryletRuntimeState`. Storage members discovered in declarations/implementations: `activated_beats`, `arc_defs`, `beat_defs`, `changes`, `choice_ids`, `choices`, `clue_ids`, `clues`, `condition_defs`, `condition_resolvers`, `consequence_defs`, `consequence_handlers`, `consequence_idempotency`, `consequence_ids`, `consequences`, `diagnostics`, `evaluation_budget_exhausted`, `evaluation_counter`, `evaluation_depth`, `evaluation_limit`, `event_executions`, `flags`, `frozen`, `journal`, `journal_epoch`, `journal_ids`, `next_change_sequence`, `objective_defs`, `objectives`, `processed_event_keys`, `revision`, `rumor_expiry_index`, `rumor_ids`, `rumors`, `storylet_defs`, `storylet_runtime`, `thread_defs`, `threads`, `variables`.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** Types: `JournalEntry`, `JournalEntryId`, `JournalEntryTypeId`, `JournalMap`, `JournalTemplate`, `JournalVisibilityState`, `NarrativeChangeBatch`, `NarrativeDiagnostics`, `RumorExpiryIndex`. Storage members: `diagnostics`, `journal`, `journal_epoch`, `journal_ids`, `next_change_sequence`, `revision`, `rumor_expiry_index`.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** `ClueId`, `JournalEntryId`, `JournalEntryTypeId`, `NarrativeArcId`, `NarrativeBeatId`, `NarrativeChoiceId`, `NarrativeChoiceOptionId`, `NarrativeConditionId`, `NarrativeConditionTypeId`, `NarrativeConsequenceExecutionId`, `NarrativeConsequenceId`, `NarrativeConsequenceTypeId`, `NarrativeEventTypeId`, `NarrativeFlagId`, `NarrativeGateId`, `NarrativeObjectiveId`, `NarrativeSceneId`, `NarrativeThreadId`, `NarrativeThreadTypeId`, `NarrativeVariableId`, `RumorId`, `StoryletId`, `TWrappedId`

- **State machines:** `ConditionEvaluationState`, `ConsequenceExecutionState`, `JournalVisibilityState`, `NarrativeChangeKind`, `NarrativeChoiceState`, `NarrativeEventExecutionPhase`, `NarrativeEventExecutionState`, `NarrativeGateState`, `NarrativeObjectiveRuntimeState`, `NarrativeObjectiveState`, `NarrativeRuntimeState`, `NarrativeThreadState`, `RumorState`, `StoryletRuntimeState`

- **Persistent and transient state:** Snapshot/checkpoint/archive carriers: `NarrativeSnapshot`. Transient request/session/queue carriers: none exposed. Durability is claimed only where the snapshot/restore contract says so.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** `8f813bdc9acb4566:epidemic::gameplay::narrative::NarrativeService::CaptureSnapshot (EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Narrative/include/Epidemic/GameFramework/Narrative/narrative.h:1020)`, `4f3f6039222c00d6:epidemic::gameplay::narrative::NarrativeService::RestoreSnapshot (EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Narrative/include/Epidemic/GameFramework/Narrative/narrative.h:1021)`

- **Threading contract:** Externally serialized state owner (normally the gameplay/simulation thread); no concurrent mutation guarantee is exposed.

### EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/NavigationSemantics

<!-- module: EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/NavigationSemantics -->

Target: `EpidemicGameFrameworkNavigationSemantics`.

- **Responsibility:** Owns semantic navigation graph/areas/links above Runtime navigation.

- **Public headers and types:** `EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/NavigationSemantics/include/Epidemic/GameFramework/NavigationSemantics/navigation_semantics.h` (`Fixed`, `INavigationCapabilityProvider`, `INavigationFactProvider`, `IdHash`, `LinkState`, `NavigationAvailability`, `NavigationChange`, `NavigationChangeBatch`, `NavigationChangeKind`, `NavigationDecisionKind`, `NavigationDiagnostics`, `NavigationDomainDefinition`, `NavigationDomainId`, `NavigationLayerId`, `NavigationLayerLifetime`, `NavigationLayerTypeId`, `NavigationLinkId`, `NavigationLinkTypeId`, `NavigationPermissionQuery`, `NavigationPermissionResult`, `NavigationReason`, `NavigationReasonId`, `NavigationRuleDefinition`, `NavigationRuleId`, `NavigationSemanticCost`, `NavigationSemanticLayer`, `NavigationSemanticLink`, `NavigationSemanticProfile`, `NavigationSemanticsService`, `NavigationSnapshot`, `RefHash`, `TraversalModeSemanticId`)

- **Dependency list:** `EpidemicGameFrameworkFoundation`, `EpidemicFoundation`

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** `INavigationCapabilityProvider`, `INavigationFactProvider`

- **Authoritative state:** Module-private storage represented by its service/runtime/record types: `LinkState`, `NavigationDomainDefinition`, `NavigationRuleDefinition`, `NavigationSemanticsService`, `NavigationSnapshot`. Storage members discovered in declarations/implementations: `capabilities`, `changes`, `cost_modified_queries`, `denied_queries`, `domains`, `dynamic_updates`, `facts`, `frozen`, `journal_epoch`, `layer_ids`, `layer_ids_by_area`, `layers`, `link_ids_by_pair`, `links`, `next_change_sequence`, `permission_queries`, `profiles`, `revision`, `rules`.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** Types: `NavigationChangeBatch`, `NavigationDiagnostics`, `NavigationPermissionQuery`. Storage members: `journal_epoch`, `next_change_sequence`, `revision`.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** `NavigationDomainId`, `NavigationLayerId`, `NavigationLayerTypeId`, `NavigationLinkId`, `NavigationLinkTypeId`, `NavigationReasonId`, `NavigationRuleId`, `TraversalModeSemanticId`

- **State machines:** `LinkState`, `NavigationChangeKind`, `NavigationDecisionKind`

- **Persistent and transient state:** Snapshot/checkpoint/archive carriers: `NavigationSnapshot`. Transient request/session/queue carriers: none exposed. Durability is claimed only where the snapshot/restore contract says so.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** `91cb3f7265e7a3bc:epidemic::gameplay::navigation_semantics::NavigationSemanticsService::CaptureSnapshot (EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/NavigationSemantics/include/Epidemic/GameFramework/NavigationSemantics/navigation_semantics.h:381)`, `4399f8b60ca36955:epidemic::gameplay::navigation_semantics::NavigationSemanticsService::RestoreSnapshot (EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/NavigationSemantics/include/Epidemic/GameFramework/NavigationSemantics/navigation_semantics.h:382)`

### EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/RolesJobs

Storage members discovered in declarations/implementations: `assignment_ids`, `assignments`, `assignments_by_worker`, `assignments_by_workplace`, `change_retention_capacity`, `changes`, `definitions`, `diagnostics`, `duties`, `duties_by_assignment`, `duties_by_subject`, `duty_ids`, `journal_epoch`, `next_change_sequence`, `revision`, `schedule_ids`, `schedules`, `schedules_by_assignment`, `shift_ids`, `terminal_duty_retention_capacity`, `workplace_ids`, `workplaces`.

### EngineFramework/IntegrationLayer/ExtendedGameplayIntegration

<!-- module: EngineFramework/IntegrationLayer/ExtendedGameplayIntegration -->

Target: `EpidemicGameFrameworkExtendedGameplayIntegration`.

- **Responsibility:** Coordinates extended item, equipment, economy and related gameplay workflows.

- **Public headers and types:** `EngineFramework/IntegrationLayer/ExtendedGameplayIntegration/include/Epidemic/GameFramework/ExtendedGameplayIntegration/extended_gameplay_adapters.h` (`CoordinatedTradeExecution`, `CoordinatedTradeGoodsExecution`, `CoordinatedTradePlan`, `CoordinatedTradeState`, `DialogueAssertKnowledgePayload`, `DialogueKnowledgeConsequenceHandler`, `DialogueShareKnowledgePayload`, `EquipmentItemsAdapter`, `ItemProcessInputProvider`, `ItemProcessOutputHandler`, `PreparedProcessItemOutputPayload`, `ProcessItemInputPayload`, `ProcessItemOutputPayload`, `ProcessItemReservationPayload`, `TradeCoordinator`, `TradeCoordinatorSnapshot`, `TradeExecutionId`, `TradeExecutionIdHash`, `TradeGoodsLegState`, `TradeGoodsLine`, `TradeMoneyLegState`)

- **Dependency list:** `EpidemicGameFrameworkItemsInventory`, `EpidemicGameFrameworkEquipment`, `EpidemicGameFrameworkDialogue`, `EpidemicGameFrameworkEconomy`, `EpidemicGameFrameworkProcesses`, `EpidemicGameFrameworkKnowledge`, `EpidemicGameFrameworkOwnership`, `EpidemicFoundation`
